Autocorr computation many times. Need helpers:
```
def next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
def autocorr_lags(a, N=None):
    n=len(a); size=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]
    # remove tiny negative
    return c
```
For gradient weights:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a, p):
    n=len(a); N=1 << (3*n-3 -1).bit_length() maybe if n>1 else 1
    w=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    if n>1:
       center=n-1
       if p[0] !=0: w[center]=2*p[0]
       idx=np.arange(1,n)
       w[center-idx]=p[idx]
       w[center+idx]=p[idx]
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
    return conv[center:center+n].copy()
```
But if n=1 off no lags; handle. Need ensure N large enough for conv length n+(2n-1)-1=3n-3. `max_len = n + (2*n-1) -1 = 3*n-3`. If max_len<=1, N=1. `N = 1 << (max_len -1).bit_length()`.
Numerical: irfft may produce tiny negative; gradient could negative due errors. Clip to 0? Since true g nonnegative for nonnegative a and p. Set `g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)`. This changes tan slightly but okay.

Soft objective:
```
def soft_values(a, tau, mode):
    c=autocorr_lags(a)
    if mode == 'off':
       c_use = c[1:].copy()
    else:
       c_use = c.copy()
    if len(c_use)==0: return inf, c, ...
    maxc = float(c_use.max())
    # if maxc <= 0? all zero, L=0
    if maxc < 1e-300:
       q = np.ones(len(c_use))/len(c_use)
       return 0.0, c, q_offset?
    shifted = (c_use - maxc)/tau
    # clip to avoid overflow
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700, 700) # shifted <=0
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    S = e.sum()
    q = e/S
    L = maxc + tau*np.log(S)
    return L, c, q
```
Need map q back to length n: q_full zeros n; if off q_full[1:]=q; else q_full=q.

But if c_use max very small, tau? okay.

def softmax_z(z):
    zm = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(np.clip(zm,-745,709))
    s=e.sum()
    return e/s
```

import numpy as np

try:

def hard_max_c(a, mode='off'):
    c = autocorr_lags(a)
    if mode == 'off':
       if len(c) <=1: return 0.0
       return float(np.max(c[1:]))
    return float(np.max(c))
def hard_score(a, mode): return 2*len(a)*hard_max_c(a,mode)
def smooth_weights(c, tau, mode): returns q_full length n
...

No nested functions.

`autocorr_lags` uses c_all[:n]. For n=1, c=[a0^2]. Off no lags -> max 0; but objective? n=1 silly. Our n large.

`gradient_from_weights`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a, p):
    n=len(a)
    if n <=1: return np.zeros_like(a)
    max_len=3*n-3
    N=next_pow2_int(max_len)
    w=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    cidx=n-1
    # p length n. p[0] may nonzero.
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0: return zeros
    if p[0] != 0:
        w[cidx] = 2.0 * p[0]
    if n > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1, n)
        # p[idx] for k=1..n-1
        if p[idx].any():
            w[cidx - idx] = p[idx]
            w[cidx + idx] = p[idx]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A*W, N)
    g = conv[cidx:cidx+n]
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)
```
Check for p length n with p[n-1]? w[cidx±(n-1)] index 0 and 2n-2 okay.
Need not use rfft of w every iter? heavy. Could optimize by computing gradient via two convolutions with W? Since w symmetric and p changes. FFT of w each iter N=16k, okay. But we also do A; A changes. For autocorr c we used A2 with N2 maybe same? For gradient N3 maybe same for n=5200. Could reuse A if same length. ```
def compute_weights(c, maxc, tau, n, mode, cu_len):
   p_use = np.empty(len(cu))
   if tau <= maxc*1e-6 or maxc <=0:
      idx = np.argmax(cu); p_use[idx]=1; else uniform
   else:
      shifted = (cu - maxc)/tau
      shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745, 0)
      e=np.exp(shifted)
      S=e.sum()
      if not finite or S<=0: p uniform
      else p=e/S
   q_full=zeros(n)
   if mode off: q_full[1:]=p_use else q_full=p_use
   return q_full, float(maxc + tau*log(S))
```

```
def stable_softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    zm = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(zm, -745, 709))
    s = e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
    return e/s
```

Pseudo-code:
```
""" ... """
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
    _HAS_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY_OPT = False

_lb_state = {'n':0,'mode':'off','tau':0.0,'N':0,'t0':0.0,'limit':0.0,'best_a':None,'best_score':float('inf')}
_global_time_budget = 1800.0

def next_pow2_int(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def as_float_array(x): ...

def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s

def make_u_shape(n, sharpness=2.0): ...
def make_spectral(n, seed=1, modes=30): ...
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n): ...

def autocorr_lags_from_a(a,N=None,n=None):
   n=len(a) if n is None else n
   if N is None:
       size=2*n-1; N=next_pow2_int(size)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
   c[:]=np.maximum(c[:n],0.0)? # but returns modified
   return c[:n].copy()
```
Maybe setting c[:n]=maximum changes small negative. For max, not necessary but for p. Could do `c = np.asarray(c[:n], dtype=np.float64); c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`.

```
def make_weight_array(n,p):
   w=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.float64)
   if n<=1:
      if n==1 and p.size>0: w[0]=2*p[0]? For off p0=0. For full, c0 derivative. But n=1 objective off no c0; full special. We'll handle.
   center=n-1
   if p.size>0 and p[0] !=0:
       w[center]=2*p[0]
   if n>1 and p.size>n? p size n:
       vals=p[1:]
       if vals.size and np.any(vals):
          idx=np.arange(1,n)
          w[center-idx]=vals
          w[center+idx]=vals
   return w
```
If p has nan? set to 0.

`gradient_from_weights(a,p,N=None)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a,p,N=None):
   n=len(a)
   if n<=1: return np.zeros(n)
   max_len=3*n-3
   if N is None: N=next_pow2_int(max_len)
   w=make_weight_array(n,p)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
   g=conv[n-1:n-1+n]
   return np.maximum(g,0.0)
```

`compute_soft_weights(c, maxc, tau, n, mode)`:
```
def compute_soft_weights(c, maxc, tau, n, mode):
    q=np.zeros(n)
    if mode == 'off':
       use_start=1; cu=c[1:]; L=0 if n<=1 else ...
    else:
       cu=c; L=...
    if len(cu)==0: return q,0.0,0.0
    maxc=float(np.max(cu))
    if maxc <= 1e-300:
       q_use=np.ones(len(cu))/len(cu)
       soft=0.0
    else:
       if tau <= maxc*1e-7:
           q_use=np.zeros(len(c_u?))
           ind=np.argmax(cu); q_use[ind]=1.0
           soft=maxc
       else:
           shifted=(cu-maxc)/tau
           shifted=np.clip(shifted,-745,0.0)
           e=np.exp(shifted)
           S=e.sum()
           if S<=0 or not finite: q_use uniform; soft=maxc
           else: q_use=e/S; soft=maxc + tau*log(S)
    if mode == 'off': q[1:]=q_use
    else: q=q_use
    return q, maxc, soft
```
Need `len(c_u?)` typo.

For L-BFGS we need similar but can inline.

Mirror function:
```
def mirror_smooth_search(a0, time_limit, mode='off', verbose=False, seed=12345, tau_frac_init=0.35, tau_frac_final=0.03, momentum_init=0.0, max_iter=1000000):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if time_limit <=0: return a0, hard_score(a0,mode)
    n=len(a0); a=norm_sum1(a0)
    N=next_pow2_int(max(2*n-1, 3*n-3))
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]
    current_cmax=hard_max_from_c(c,mode) # no trial
    best_cmax=current_cmax; best_a=a.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    alpha_scale=1.0
    success_count=0
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    step_prev=np.zeros(n)
    momentum=0.0
    last_print=t0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it +=1
       if current_cmax <= 0: break
       # tau fraction
       progress = min(1.0, it / 5000.0 + (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(1.0,time_limit)*0.5)
       frac = tau_frac_final + (tau_frac_init - tau_frac_final) * math.exp(-it / 800.0)
       frac = max(tau_frac_final, min(tau_frac_init, frac))
       tau = max(current_cmax * frac, 1e-300)
       q, maxc_q, soft = compute_soft_weights(c, current_cmax, tau, n, mode)
       # gradient
       g=gradient_from_weights(a, q, N)
       mean_g=float(np.dot(a,g))
       raw = g - mean_g
       # momentum maybe after some successes
       if momentum >0:
          step = momentum*step_prev + (1-momentum)*raw
       else:
          step = raw
       step_scale=float(np.max(np.abs(step))) if step.size else 0
       if not finite or step_scale <=1e-300:
           a=best_a.copy(); c=...; step_prev=0; continue
       # candidate alphas
       alphas_base = np.array([0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0])
       alphas = np.unique(np.clip(alphas_base*alpha_scale, 1e-4, 200.0))
       # maybe if alpha_scale small add 0.1
       best_trial_cmax = current_cmax
       best_trial_a = None
       best_soft = soft
       for alpha in alphas:
          if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit: break
          exp_arg = -float(alpha) * step / step_scale
          b = a * np.exp(np.clip(exp_arg, -40.0, 40.0))
          s=b.sum()
          if s <= 1e-300: continue
          b = np.maximum(b,0.0); b/=b.sum()
          trial_cmax = hard_max_trial(b,N,n,mode)
          # compute trial soft? optional. We can if using soft tie.
          if trial_cmax < best_trial_cmax - 1e-18 or (trial_cmax <= best_trial_cmax + 1e-14 and False):
              best_trial_cmax = trial_cmax; best_trial_a=b
       if best_trial_a is not None:
          a=best_trial_a
          A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]; current_cmax=hard_max_from_c(c,mode)
          if current_cmax < best_cmax - 1e-18:
              best_cmax=current_cmax; best_a=a.copy()
              no_improve=0
              success_count +=1
              if success_count > 20 and momentum < 0.3: momentum=0.3
              alpha_scale = min(alpha_scale*1.05, 100.0)
              step_prev = step
          else:
              # accepted current decreased but not best? possible due max change? set?
              no_improve +=1
              step_prev = step
       else:
          # fail
          no_improve +=1
          alpha_scale = max(alpha_scale*0.65, 1e-3)
          step_prev = step_prev * 0.0  # reset momentum?
          momentum = 0.0
          # if no improve too much, perturb
          if no_improve >= 300:
              # random perturbation from best
              noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
              trial = a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -4,4))
              trial = np.maximum(trial,0); ss=trial.sum(); if ss>0 trial/=ss
              tc=hard_max_trial(trial,N,n,mode)
              if tc < current_cmax:
                  a=trial; recompute; alpha_scale=1.0; no_improve=0
              elif tc < best_cmax:
                  a=trial; recompute; best_cmax=tc; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
              else:
                  a=best_a.copy(); A=...; c=...; current_cmax=best_cmax; step_prev=0
              no_improve=0
          elif no_improve >= 100:
              alpha_scale=1.0
              # smaller deterministic tau? no, frac maybe.
       if verbose and time.monotonic()-t0-last_print > 20:
           last_print=time.monotonic()
           print(f"  mirror n={n} mode={mode} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s J={2*n*best_cmax:.6f} stall={no_improve} alpha={alpha_scale:.2f}")
    # final maybe coordinate? no
    return best_a, 2*n*best_cmax
```

```
def _lbfgs_objective(z):
    st=_lb_state
    n=st['n']; N=st['N']; mode=st['mode']; tau=float(st['tau'])
    a=stable_softmax(z)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    if mode=='off':
        cu=c[1:]
        hard = float(np.max(cu)) if cu.size else 0.0
    else:
        cu=c
        hard=float(np.max(c))
    score=2*n*hard
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < st.get('best_score', float('inf')):
        st['best_a']=a.copy(); st['best_score']=score
    if len(cu)==0: return 0.0
    maxc=hard if mode=='full' else hard? Wait hard for off is max c[1:], same maxc. For full, max c includes c0; maxc=hard. Use hard.
    if maxc <= 1e-300: return 0.0
    if tau <= maxc * 1e-8:
       return float(maxc)
    shifted = (cu - maxc) / tau
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745, 0)
    e=np.exp(shifted); S=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <=0: return float(maxc)
    return float(maxc + tau * math.log(S))
```

def _lbfgs_grad(z):
   st=_lb_state; n=st['n']; N=st['N']; mode=st['mode']; tau=st['tau']
   a=stable_softmax(z)
   A=... c...

   maxc=float(np.max(cu))

       q_full=np.zeros(n); idx = int(np.argmax(cu)); q_full[idx]=1
   else:
       shifted...; p=e/S
       q_full...
   g=gradient_from_weights(a,q_full,N)
   mean=dot(a,g)
   hz=a*(g-mean)
   # Clip gradient to avoid inf
   hz=np.nan_to_num(hz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

`lbfgs_polish`:
```
def lbfgs_polish(a0, time_limit, mode='off', verbose=True):
    if not _HAS_SCIPY_OPT or time_limit < 20: return a0, hard_score(a0,mode)
    n=len(a0); a=norm_sum1(a0)
    base_score=2*n*hard_max_c(a,mode)
    N=next_pow2_int(max(2*n-1, 3*n-3))
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)); z=z-z.mean()
    st=_lb_state
    st['n']=n; st['N']=N; st['mode']=mode
    st['best_a']=a.copy(); st['best_score']=base_score
    t0=time.monotonic()
    frac_list = [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
    # allocate time per frac maybe decreasing
    for idx, frac in enumerate(frac_list):
        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t0)
        if rem < 15: break
        cmax=hard_max_c(a,mode)
        tau = max(cmax*frac, 1e-300)
        slice_t = min(rem, 120.0)  # each up to 120
        if idx==0: slice_t = min(rem, 180.0)
        st['tau']=tau
        st['t0']=time.monotonic()
        st['limit']=slice_t
        st['best_a']=a.copy(); st['best_score']=2*n*hard_max_c(a,mode)
        try:
           res=scipy_minimize(_lbfgs_objective, z, jac=_lbfgs_grad, method='L-BFGS-B',
                 options={'maxiter':200, 'maxls':8, 'gtol':1e-9, 'ftol':1e-12},
                 callback=_lbfgs_stop_callback)
           if hasattr(res,'x'): z=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception as e:
           if verbose: print('  L-BFGS error', e)
        a_new=stable_softmax(z)
        a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0); ss=a_new.sum(); if ss>0: a_new/=ss
        score_new=2*n*hard_max_c(a_new,mode)
        if score_new < st['best_score']:
            a=a_new.copy(); st['best_a']=a.copy(); st['best_score']=score_new
        else:
            if st['best_a'] is not None:
                a=st['best_a'].copy()
        if verbose: print(f"  L-BFGS tau frac={frac} score={2*n*hard_max_c(a,mode)/ (2*n)?}...")
    # return best from state and a
    best_a = st.get('best_a') if st.get('best_a') is not None else a
    if best_a is None: best_a=a
    return best_a, 2*n*hard_max_c(best_a,mode)
```

`spectral_init(n,n_modes=40,seed=0):`

rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

for f in range(1,n_modes+1):
   c=rng.normal(0,0.2/math.sqrt(f))
   ph=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)

a=np.maximum(a,1e-6)

```

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f=np.asarray(f,float); n=len(f)
    if new_n==n: return f.copy()
    s=f.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    f=f/s
    old_x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    out=np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    ss=out.sum(); if ss<=0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    return out/ss
```
For nonuniform? okay.

`autocorr_lags_from_a(a,N=None)`:
```
def autocorr_lags_from_a(a,N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if N is None:
       N=next_pow2_int(max(1,2*n-1))
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
If a contains nan? norm assures no. Could `np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0, posinf=0)`? Not needed. But for safety in hard_score:
```
a=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a,float), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```

`hard_max_from_c(c,mode)`:
```
def hard_max_from_c(c, mode='off'):
   if c.size==0: return 0.0
   if mode == 'off':
      if c.size <=1: return 0.0
      return float(np.max(c[1:]))
   return float(np.max(c))
```

`hard_max_trial(a_trial,N,n,mode)`:
```
def hard_max_trial(a_trial,N,n,mode):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a_trial,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]
   if c.size ==0: return 0.0
   # max without array max? For off:
   if mode=='off':
      if n<=1: return 0.0
      val=float(np.max(c[1:]))
   else:
      val=float(np.max(c))
   if val < 0: val=0.0
   return val
```
Use c not clipped all; max negative? can clip val.

`make_weight_array(n,p)`: Need p length n, nonnegative.
```
def make_weight_array(n,p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if p.size > n: p=p[:n]
    elif p.size < n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n); tmp[:p.size]=p; p=tmp
    w=np.zeros(2*n-1,float)
    if n <=0: return w
    center=n-1
    if p[0] > 0:
       w[center]=2.0*p[0]
    if n > 1:
       vals=p[1:]
       # if any vals > 0
       idx=np.arange(1,n)
       w[center-idx]=vals
       w[center+idx]=vals
    return w
```
No need any. Assign zeros okay.

`gradient_from_weights(a,p,N=None)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(a,p,N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1:
       return np.zeros(n,float)
    max_len=3*n-3
    if N is None or N < max_len:
       N=next_pow2_int(max_len)
    w=make_weight_array(n,p)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
    g=conv[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    return g
```

`compute_soft_weights(c, current_maxc, tau, n, mode)`:
Need pass c length n. Returns q_full, maxc, soft.
```
def compute_soft_weights(c, tau, n, mode):
    q=np.zeros(n,float)
    if mode=='off':
       if n<=1: return q,0.0,0.0
       cu=c[1:]
       start=1
    else:
       cu=c
       start=0
    if cu.size==0: return q,0.0,0.0
    maxc=float(np.max(cu))
    if maxc <= 1e-300:
       q_use=np.ones(cu.size)/cu.size
       q[start:]=q_use
       return q,0.0,0.0
    if tau <= maxc*1e-8:
       idx=int(np.argmax(cu))
       q_use=np.zeros(cu.size); q_use[idx]=1.0
       q[start:]=q_use
       return q,maxc,maxc
    shifted=(cu-maxc)/tau
    shifted=np.clip(shifted,-745.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(shifted)
    S=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
       idx=int(np.argmax(cu))
       q_use=np.zeros(cu.size); q_use[idx]=1.0
       q[start:]=q_use
       return q,maxc,maxc
    q_use=e/S
    q[start:]=q_use
    soft=maxc + tau*math.log(S)
    return q,maxc,soft
```
If c contains nan? should not. But if shift overflow? exponent <=0, no.

```
def finalize_best(candidates, time_budget, verbose=True):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    best_a = candidates[0].copy() if candidates else None
    best_actual = float('inf')
    seen=[]
    for idx,a in enumerate(candidates):
       a=np.asarray(a,float)
       if a.size==0: continue
       # dedup
       dup=False
       for b in seen:
          if b.size==a.size:
             diff=np.linalg.norm(a-b)
             if diff < 1e-10: dup=True; break
       if dup: continue
       seen.append(a.copy())
       if len(seen)>15: 
          # evaluate remaining? maybe still? We can break to save
          # But if many, evaluate only top? We'll just break.
          # But best candidate might later. We can evaluate all up to 15.
          if len(seen)>15: break
       rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
       if rem < 5: break
       try:
          arr=[float(x) for x in a.tolist()]
          sc=evaluate_sequence(arr)
       except Exception:
          sc=float('inf')
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_actual:
          best_actual=sc; best_a=a.copy()
          if verbose: print(f"  finalize candidate {idx}: actual={sc:.6f}, n={a.size}")
    if not np.isfinite(best_actual):
       # fallback to first unique internal best? choose first
       best_a = seen[0].copy() if seen else best_a
    return best_a, best_actual
```

Coordinate polish idea:
- Given a, compute active top K lags (e.g., K=30) with c_k high.
- Build "sensitivity" `sens[i] = sum_{active k} a_{i±k} weighted by c_k` or just indicator in active windows.
- For each active lag k, for a few high pair indices i where a_i a_{i+k} is high, move a small fraction of mass from i (or i+k) to a low-sensitivity destination j (not in active windows) and test hard cmax over all top? Evaluating full max each trial expensive but okay 60s.
- Use random/low destination candidates: top 3 low sens.
- Fractions [0.01,0.05,0.1]. If improves best, accept.
- If no improve, random moves.

def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, mode, seed=1234):
    n=len(a0); a=norm_sum1; best=...
    N=...
    while time:
       A,c,recompute current cmax

       g=gradient_from_weights(a,q,N)
       # source candidates: top 20 indices with a_i*g_i high? Since decreasing them reduces active.
       # dest candidates: bottom 20 indices with a_i low and g low.
       src_idx = np.argsort(a*g)[- 5:]

       if no improve many, perturb.
```

Now, coordinate_polish implementation details. Let's write:
```
def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, mode='off', verbose=False, seed=1234):
    n=len(a0); a=norm_sum1(a0)
    if n<=1: return a, hard_score
    N=next_pow2_int(max(1,3*n-3))
    A,c,cur=...
    best_a=a.copy(); best_c=cur; t0=...
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    alpha? fracs = np.array([0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1])
    stall=0; it=0
    while time<limit:
       it+=1
       # ensure a current
       # compute active weights top K
       if mode off: cu=c[1:], offset=1 else cu=c, offset=0
       if cu.size==0: break
       # top K
       K=min(30, cu.size)
       top = np.argsort(cu)[::-1][:K]
       vals = cu[top]
       maxv=vals[0]
       if maxv <=0: break
       tau=max(maxv*0.15,1e-300)
       shifted=(vals-maxv)/tau; e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-745,0)); S=e.sum(); p=e/S
       q=np.zeros(n)
       idx_full = offset + top
       q[idx_full]=p
       g=gradient_from_weights(a,q,N)
       score = a*g
       # source candidates top score, dest bottom score
       src = np.argsort(score)[-6:]
       dst = np.argsort(score)[:6]
       found=False
       # random destination low score too?
       for src_i in src:
          if score[src_i] <= 1e-20: continue
          for dst_i in dst:
             if dst_i == src_i: continue
             for frac in fracs:
                 if time exceeded break
                 amt = score? no amt = a[src_i]*frac
                 if amt <= 0: continue
                 trial = a.copy()
                 trial[src_i] -= amt
                 if trial[src_i] < 0: continue
                 trial[dst_i] += amt
                 s=trial.sum(); if s<=0 continue; trial/=s
                 tc=hard_max_trial(trial,N,n,mode)
                 if tc < best_c - 1e-18:
                     a=trial; A... c... cur=tc; best_c=tc; best_a=a.copy(); found=True; break loops
          if found: break
       if found: stall=0
       else:
          stall += 1
          # try random move if stall >20
          if stall > 20:
             i = rng.integers(n); j = rng.integers(n)
             if i!=j and a[i]>0:
                trial=a.copy(); frac=rng.uniform(0.002,0.02); amt=a[i]*frac; trial[i]-=amt; trial[j]+=amt; normalize; tc=...
                if tc < best_c: accept else if tc < cur*1.001: maybe accept current=trial
          if stall > 200:
             # reset from best + noise
             a = best_a.copy(); recompute; stall=0
             noise = rng.normal(0,0.05,n); trial=a*exp(noise); normalize; tc
             if tc <= best_c*1.005: a=trial; recompute; else a=best_a; recompute
       if verbose and print_interval...
    return best_a, 2*n*best_c
```

`recompute_ac`:
```
def recompute_ac(a,N,n,mode):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:n]
    cm=hard_max_from_c(c,mode)
    return A,c,cm
```

`compute_soft_weights(c, tau)`:
```
def compute_soft_weights(c, tau):
    L=c.size
    if L==0: return np.zeros(0),0.0,0.0
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <=1e-300:
        return np.ones(L)/L,0.0,0.0
    if tau <= maxc*1e-8:
       p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return p,maxc,maxc
    shifted=(c-maxc)/tau; shifted=np.clip(shifted,-745,0); e=np.exp(shifted); S=e.sum();
    if S<=0 or not finite: p onehot
    p=e/S; soft=maxc + tau*log(S); return p,maxc,soft
```

`gradient_from_conv_weights(a,p,N=None)`: p len L = 2n-1. If p size mismatch, pad/trim.
```
def gradient_from_conv_weights(a,p,N=None):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if n<=1: return np.zeros(n)
   p=np.asarray(p); if p.size != L: adjust
   if p.size > L: p=p[:L] else pad
   max_len=3*n-3
   if N is None or N<max_len: N=next_pow2_int(max_len)
   pr=p[::-1]
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); P=np.fft.rfft(pr,N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,N)
   g=2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
   return g
```
Check p[::-1] for p length L. For n=3, good. For p with uniform weights, gradient? Should be okay.

Let's design weight computation:
```
def compute_top_weights(c, tau, top_frac=0.2, n_top_max=2000):
   L=len(c); K = min(L, max(1, int(round(L*top_frac)), maybe min(L, max(50, 100?))))
   # Use top indices by c values
   if K >= L: idx = arange(L); vals=c
   else: idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]; vals=c[idx]
   maxv=vals[0]
   if maxv <=0: uniform over idx
   elif tau <= maxv*1e-8: p_top onehot argmax(vals)
   else: p_top=softmax((vals-maxv)/tau)
   p=zeros(L); p[idx]=p_top
   return p,maxv,soft? soft approx maybe maxv + tau log(sum exp over idx)
```

Implement `compute_active_weights(c, tau, top_frac=1.0):`
```
def compute_active_weights(c, tau, top_frac=1.0):
    L = c.size
    if L == 0: return np.zeros(0),0.0,0.0
    frac = float(top_frac)
    if frac <= 0: frac = 1e-6
    K = int(round(L * frac))
    if K < 1: K=1
    if K > L: K=L
    if K == L:
        vals = c
        idx = None
    else:
        # argpartition; if K close L okay
        top = np.argpartition(c, L - K)[L - K:]
        vals = c[top]
        idx = top
    maxv = float(np.max(vals))
    if maxv <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L); p_top=np.ones(K)/K; if idx is None: p=p_top else p[idx]=p_top; return p,0.0,0.0
    if K == 1 or tau <= maxv * 1e-8:
        p = np.zeros(L); j=int(np.argmax(vals)); p_idx = j if idx is None else idx[j]; p[p_idx]=1.0; return p,maxv,maxv
    shifted=(vals-maxv)/tau
    shifted=np.clip(shifted,-745,0.0)
    e=np.exp(shifted)
    S=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
       p=zeros; j=argmax(vals); p_idx...; return p,maxv,maxv
    p_top=e/S
    p=np.zeros(L)
    if idx is None: p=p_top
    else: p[idx]=p_top
    soft=maxv + tau*math.log(S)
    return p, maxv, soft
```
When K=L and idx None, `p=p_top` length L. Good. If K small, `idx` unsorted. Fine.

`objective` for mirror uses maxc from weights (max over active, which is global max if K>=1). Good.

```
def make_greedy_conv(n, factor=0.8):
    M = factor / n
    a=np.zeros(n); b=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    # order outside-in by increasing number of existing constraints? endpoints then next.
    order=[]
    d=0
    while len(order)<n:
       if d < n: order.append(d)
       if n-1-d >=0 and len(order)<n: order.append(n-1-d)
       d += 1
    active=[]
    for i in order:
       if a[i] > 1e-300: continue
       # self constraint if b[i] < M
       max_x = math.sqrt(max(0.0, M - b[i]))
       if max_x <= 0: continue? try small
       if len(active)>0:
          nz=np.array(active)
          ai=a[nz]
          # For each existing j, coefficient i+j
          ratios = (M - b[i + nz]) / (2.0 * ai)
          # also coefficients nz+i same? b symmetric? Convolution ordered b not symmetric but due same a maybe not; need constraints for b[i+j] and b[j+i] if i != j? Adding x at i: for each existing j, b[i+j] += x a_j and b[j+i] += x a_j if i!=j (j+i same index? i+j = j+i, same coefficient! Wait b index is sum i+j, so only one index changes by 2x a_j? Earlier polynomial convolution: term i+j and j+i both contribute to same coefficient i+j, total 2x a_j. The existing b[i+j] includes any products. So constraint uses b[i+j] and update once with 2x a_j. But if j existing and there were prior term? yes. So ratios from b[i+nz].
          min_ratio = np.min(ratios)
          if finite and min_ratio < max_x: max_x = min_ratio
       # also remaining? no
       max_x *= 0.95
       if max_x <= 1e-12: continue? maybe set 1e-12
       a[i] = max_x
       if len(active)>0:
          nz=np.array(active); ai=a[nz]
          b[i+nz] += max_x * ai * 2.0  # ordered pairs i-j and j-i
          # But if some j? No self since i not active.
       else:
          b[i+i] += max_x*max_x
       active.append(i)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return make_u_shape(n,2.5)
    return a/s
```

`_lbfgs_objective`:
```
st=_lb_state; n=st['n']; N=st['N']; tau=st['tau']; top_frac=st.get('top_frac',1.0)

A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:size]; c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
cmax=float(np.max(c)) if size else 0

if finite score < st['best_score']: st['best_a']=a.copy(); st['best_score']=score
if size==0: return 0
if cmax <= 1e-300: return 0
if tau <= cmax*1e-8: return cmax
shifted=(c-cmax)/tau; clip; e; S; if bad return cmax
return cmax + tau*log(S)
```

Now, let's write `mirror_smooth_search` carefully:
```
def mirror_smooth_search(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=1, tau_frac_init=0.12, tau_frac_final=0.01, top_frac_init=0.5, top_frac_final=0.03, tau_decay=1000.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=...; a=norm_sum1(a0)
    n=len(a); N=next_pow2_int(max(1,3*n-3))
    A,c,cur = recompute_conv(a,N,n)
    best_c=cur; best_a=a.copy()
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    alpha_scale=1.0
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    last_print=t0
    step_prev = None? skip
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < total:
        it +=1
        if n <=1 or cur <= 1e-300: break
        anneal = math.exp(-it / max(1.0, tau_decay))
        tau_frac = max(tau_frac_final, tau_frac_final + (tau_frac_init-tau_frac_final)*anneal)
        top_frac = max(top_frac_final, top_frac_final + (top_frac_init-top_frac_final)*anneal)
        tau = max(cur * tau_frac, 1e-300)
        # tie-break
        if cur > 0:
            c_w = c + cur * 1e-12 * rng.normal(size=c.size)
            c_w = np.maximum(c_w, 0.0) # maybe
        else: c_w=c
        p, maxv, _ = compute_active_weights(c_w, tau, top_frac)
        g = gradient_from_conv_weights(a, p, N)
        mean = float(np.dot(a,g))
        raw = g - mean
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(raw))) if raw.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-15:
            # try perturb
            ...
            continue
        alphas = np.array([0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]) * alpha_scale
        alphas = np.unique(np.clip(alphas, 1e-3, 200.0))
        best_trial_c = cur
        best_trial_a = None
        for alpha in alphas:
            ex = -float(alpha) * raw / scale
            b = a * np.exp(np.clip(ex, -40.0, 40.0))
            ss=b.sum()
            if ss <= 1e-300: continue
            b=b/ss
            tc=hard_max_trial(b,N,n)
            if tc < best_trial_c - 1e-18:
                best_trial_c=tc; best_trial_a=b
        if best_trial_a is not None:
            a=best_trial_a; A,c,cur=recompute_conv(...)
            if cur < best_c - 1e-18:
                best_c=cur; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
                alpha_scale = min(alpha_scale*1.05, 50.0)
            else:
                # current improved but best not? possible if cur > old best? No if old cur>best. But accept.
                no_improve +=1
                alpha_scale = min(alpha_scale*1.1, 50.0)? maybe not.
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            alpha_scale = max(alpha_scale*0.6, 1e-3)
            if no_improve >= 200:
                # perturb from best
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.01*min(5, no_improve//200)
                a = best_a.copy(); A,c,bc=recompute_conv
                cur=bc
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                trial = a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -5,5))
                ss=trial.sum(); if ss>0 trial/=ss
                tc=hard_max_trial(trial,N,n)
                if tc < cur: a=trial; cur=tc; if cur<best: update
                elif tc < best_c * 1.005 and rng.random()<0.5:
                    a=trial; cur=tc
                else:
                    a=best_a.copy(); A,c,cur=recompute_conv(...)
                no_improve=0; alpha_scale=1.0
        # if no_improve very high >1000, larger perturbation or break?
        if verbose and ... print
    return best_a, 2.0*n*best_c
```

```
def stable_softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-1e300, posinf=1e300, neginf=-1e300)
    m=np.max(z)
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z-m,-745,709))
    s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return ones
    return e/s
```

```
def make_greedy_conv(n, factor=1.0):
    M = max(factor / max(n,1), 1e-12)
    a = np.zeros(n); b = np.zeros(2*n-1)
    active = []
    order=[]; d=0
    while len(order)<n:
        if d<n: order.append(d)
        if n-1-d>=0 and len(order)<n: order.append(n-1-d)
        d += 1
    for i in order:
        if a[i] > 0: continue
        max_x = math.sqrt(max(0.0, M - b[2*i]))
        if active:
            nz = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
            ai = a[nz]
            # exclude tiny
            good = ai > 1e-300
            if good.any():
                nzg = nz[good]; aig=ai[good]
                idx = i + nzg
                ratios = (M - b[idx]) / (2.0 * aig)
                finite = np.isfinite(ratios) & (ratios > 0.0)
                if finite.any():
                    max_x = min(max_x, float(np.min(ratios[finite])))
        max_x *= 0.95
        if max_x <= 1e-12:
            continue
        a[i] = max_x
        b[2*i] += max_x * max_x
        if active:
            nz = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
            ai = a[nz]
            idx = i + nz
            b[idx] += 2.0 * max_x * ai
        active.append(i)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-8:
        return make_u_shape(n, 2.5)
    return a/s
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The optimizer uses:
- FFT-based convolution coefficients b = a * a
- Smoothed active-set mirror descent / multiplicative gradient steps
- L-BFGS polishing of a soft-logsumexp surrogate (via scipy if available)
- Fast coordinate polish
- Short length exploration and final validation with evaluate_sequence

All helper functions are top-level and no lambda/nested functions are used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_optimize
    _HAS_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_SCIPY_OPT = False

_lb_state = {
    "n": 0,
    "N": 0,
    "tau": 1e-300,
    "t0": 0.0,
    "limit": 0.0,
}


def next_pow2_int(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def stable_softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-1e300, posinf=1e300, neginf=-1e300)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones_z:k = energy factored velvet build Medieval online reimbursement)casua
